### Identity (reviewed)

Native Tetris implementation in C, with assembly graphics, keyboard and PT3 music routines.
- https://github.com/6502Nerd/Oric-Tetris/blob/a2a792f3897c58968cb773d9c298471ac716358d/README.md
- https://github.com/6502Nerd/Oric-Tetris/blob/a2a792f3897c58968cb773d9c298471ac716358d/tetris.c

### Classification (reviewed)

tetris.c implements piece rotation, collision, line removal and main play state. PT3 song files and generated song assembly are music data, not substituted game executables. No explicit whole-project licence conclusion established from bounded inspection.
- https://github.com/6502Nerd/Oric-Tetris/blob/a2a792f3897c58968cb773d9c298471ac716358d/README.md
- https://github.com/6502Nerd/Oric-Tetris/blob/a2a792f3897c58968cb773d9c298471ac716358d/tetris.c

Documented output/support targets: Oric. Processor scope: 6502.

### Build (reviewed)

osdk_config.bat names TETRIS at $800 and compiles tetris, tetris_data, dflat_lib, pt3_lib and music resources; README documents OSDK build and emulator launch. No upstream source was compiled or executed. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.
- https://github.com/6502Nerd/Oric-Tetris/blob/a2a792f3897c58968cb773d9c298471ac716358d/README.md
- https://github.com/6502Nerd/Oric-Tetris/blob/a2a792f3897c58968cb773d9c298471ac716358d/tetris.c

### Identity (reviewed)

Davide Bucci original cavern-flight game for an unexpanded VIC-20.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/vic20-cavern-exp/blob/c0d9e9a16479964a13d72c87983ffcedc6f2b596/README.md
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/vic20-cavern-exp/blob/c0d9e9a16479964a13d72c87983ffcedc6f2b596/explorer.s

### Classification (reviewed)

66KB labelled source implements movement, scrolling cavern drawing, collisions, custom character graphics and sound. Included raw_screen_data_intro.s is intro image data rather than an opaque game engine. GPL-3.0 licence file present in pinned tree; no separate asset-rights determination made.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/vic20-cavern-exp/blob/c0d9e9a16479964a13d72c87983ffcedc6f2b596/README.md
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/vic20-cavern-exp/blob/c0d9e9a16479964a13d72c87983ffcedc6f2b596/explorer.s

### Identity (reviewed)

Original author Andy Frigaard recovered Bird Strike sources from a 1984 disk, with a modern BeebAsm restoration and separately cleaned/enhanced source.
- https://github.com/qurm/BirdStrike/blob/b13704515ac3996feeacb220ab03b2a1ba6c6869/README.md
- https://github.com/qurm/BirdStrike/blob/b13704515ac3996feeacb220ab03b2a1ba6c6869/Build/GG-02.asm

### Classification (reviewed)

This is primarily recovered original BBC BASIC/assembler source, with small formerly binary-only code sections reconstructed by disassembly. Clean branch relocates code, integrates patches, optimizes and fixes level progression. Build/GG-02.asm contains substantive labelled gameplay routines. Graphics G-Note.bin, G-Plane.bin and X.bin are identified by author as sprites/data. GPL-3.0 root licence file present; historical game and component attribution retained.
- https://github.com/qurm/BirdStrike/blob/b13704515ac3996feeacb220ab03b2a1ba6c6869/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Preserved Mark Reid Getaway! source adapted for modern ATasm assembly, including labelled 1982 gameplay and data definitions.
- https://github.com/playermissile/getaway/blob/c7df8c611f0fba69cfb8f4e3da6cc500c420d622/src/getaway.asm
- https://github.com/playermissile/getaway/blob/c7df8c611f0fba69cfb8f4e3da6cc500c420d622/src/getaway.dat

### Classification (reviewed)

getaway.asm contains extensive car movement, chase, radar, collision, scoring and display code. getaway.dat is included assembly data definitions; playfield.dat/debug1.dat/debug2.dat are map data imported with INCBIN. No wholesale opaque executable inclusion found. Source embeds credit thanking Mark Reid for public-domain release; attribution is recorded rather than independently legal-verified. Source thanks original author for public-domain release; no separate root licence in pinned tree.

### Identity (reviewed)

Coherent collection of native ProDOS-8 clock, RAM-disk, accelerator and selector drivers with shared network-aware SYSTEM-file chaining.
- https://github.com/a2stuff/prodos-drivers/blob/9d040dae2136f9ef9343490ee1c9a2e0e0ffe169/README.md
- https://github.com/a2stuff/prodos-drivers/blob/9d040dae2136f9ef9343490ee1c9a2e0e0ffe169/clocks/cricket/cricket.system.s

### Classification (reviewed)

Cricket driver explicitly sets .setcpu 6502 and implements hardware detection and ProDOS clock installation. driver_preamble.inc provides actual next-SYSTEM-file search/chaining. Historical driver originals are attributed and adapted, rather than merely redistributed binary blobs. No explicit whole-project licence conclusion established from bounded inspection.
- https://github.com/a2stuff/prodos-drivers/blob/9d040dae2136f9ef9343490ee1c9a2e0e0ffe169/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Native ProDOS file-management utility suite centered on Sortdir directory sorting, filesystem checking/repair and ProDOS 2.5 compatibility conversion, with auxiliary network/storage tools.
- https://github.com/bobbimanners/ProDOS-Utils/blob/373eab52c988a3862d34d994d6b2aba2e8c92b62/README.md
- https://github.com/bobbimanners/ProDOS-Utils/blob/373eab52c988a3862d34d994d6b2aba2e8c92b62/README-sortdir.md

### Classification (reviewed)

sortdir.c is substantive approximately 70KB filesystem implementation with volume/directory parsing, checking and sorting, not a wrapper around a supplied executable. README separately lists VEServer Python service, Extend and Ultrawarp clock adaptation; these are related suite components. Root LICENSE is BSD-2-Clause; Makefile header says GPL v3+. Preserve discrepancy rather than assigning an unqualified suite-wide licence.
- https://github.com/bobbimanners/ProDOS-Utils/blob/373eab52c988a3862d34d994d6b2aba2e8c92b62/README.md
- https://github.com/bobbimanners/ProDOS-Utils/blob/373eab52c988a3862d34d994d6b2aba2e8c92b62/README-sortdir.md

Inspected initialization, assembler-procedure calls, memory-dependent music selection, game loop and resource loading in 14k-line source. Original-source preservation and compatibility repair, not a binary-derived decompilation. No explicit general license file; README invites modification/forks, which is not a standardized license.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native source port of id Software linuxdoom to classic Macintosh System 7, with monochrome, grayscale/color video and SE/30-specific performance changes.
- https://github.com/retrospectmike/MacDoomSE30/blob/01d5459c275e156d38dd16c53c6c33b273fdde10/README.md
- https://github.com/retrospectmike/MacDoomSE30/blob/01d5459c275e156d38dd16c53c6c33b273fdde10/src/CMakeLists.txt

### Classification (reviewed)

Substantial actual Mac platform layer: QuickDraw/CopyBits path avoids 24-bit NuBus-address hazard, direct mono framebuffer rendering, dithering/colormap lookup changes. Reuses released Doom engine source; not a new Doom decompilation. GPLv2 top-level LICENSE present, while legacy Doom headers still mention the older Doom Source Code License; preserve notices and review inherited license history before redistribution. IWAD assets separately required and not covered by engine source terms.
- https://github.com/retrospectmike/MacDoomSE30/blob/01d5459c275e156d38dd16c53c6c33b273fdde10/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Partial Linux X68000 development harness with freestanding C/F-line DOS calls, raw-to-Human68k X-header wrapper and MAME/Lua test automation.
- https://github.com/Armitage64/x68000dev/blob/4cc02e9060a90f91a31c6b13d6d9e830a367b828/README.md
- https://github.com/Armitage64/x68000dev/blob/4cc02e9060a90f91a31c6b13d6d9e830a367b828/Makefile

### Classification (reviewed)

Custom .X wrapper zeroes relocation/BSS/symbol sizes and wraps entire payload as text; valid only for suitable freestanding position-independent example, not a general ELF relocator. Test harness patches MAME cfg, swaps AUTOEXEC and samples VRAM; claims of passing tests remain author claims. No explicit top-level license found; bundled tools and boot media have separate provenance/rights.
- https://github.com/Armitage64/x68000dev/blob/4cc02e9060a90f91a31c6b13d6d9e830a367b828/README.md
- https://github.com/Armitage64/x68000dev/blob/4cc02e9060a90f91a31c6b13d6d9e830a367b828/Makefile

### Source CPU (reviewed)

Documented source/input platforms: Sharp X68000, Human68k. Processor scope: m68k.
- https://github.com/Armitage64/x68000dev/blob/4cc02e9060a90f91a31c6b13d6d9e830a367b828/README.md
- https://github.com/Armitage64/x68000dev/blob/4cc02e9060a90f91a31c6b13d6d9e830a367b828/Makefile

### Target CPU (reviewed)

Documented output/support targets: Linux, Sharp X68000, Human68k. Processor scope: m68k.
- https://github.com/Armitage64/x68000dev/blob/4cc02e9060a90f91a31c6b13d6d9e830a367b828/README.md
- https://github.com/Armitage64/x68000dev/blob/4cc02e9060a90f91a31c6b13d6d9e830a367b828/Makefile

### Build (reviewed)

Default make all references src/helloa.s, missing at pinned commit (src has only helloc.c/crt0.s); default build incomplete by static inspection. C path uses m68k-linux-gnu-gcc -m68000 -mpcrel, ELF objcopy and 64-byte HU header. Bundled vasm binary present but not run. No compilation or execution performed. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.
- https://github.com/Armitage64/x68000dev/blob/4cc02e9060a90f91a31c6b13d6d9e830a367b828/README.md
- https://github.com/Armitage64/x68000dev/blob/4cc02e9060a90f91a31c6b13d6d9e830a367b828/Makefile

### Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found)

Requires Linux/MAME, mtools, BIOS, Human68k disk; documented xdotool fallback. Test automation changes local disk/config, which was not performed.
- https://github.com/Armitage64/x68000dev/blob/4cc02e9060a90f91a31c6b13d6d9e830a367b828/README.md
- https://github.com/Armitage64/x68000dev/blob/4cc02e9060a90f91a31c6b13d6d9e830a367b828/Makefile

README/project description calls this a Claude Code test harness and setup documents Claude Code integration, but extent of generated implementation is not established.

### Identity (reviewed)

Browser-based classic Macintosh C/Rez development playground integrating wasm Retro68 tools, resource-fork handling, HFS image patching and emulated System 7 deployment.
- https://github.com/khawkins98/classic-vibe-mac/blob/0b7a51a8b2c2fc2eda4f21a56483e210744ca7d8/README.md
- https://github.com/khawkins98/classic-vibe-mac/blob/0b7a51a8b2c2fc2eda4f21a56483e210744ca7d8/package.json

### Classification (reviewed)

Substantive glue beyond emulator embedding: HFS patcher inserts a MacBinary app into fixed empty template by catalog/bitmap/MDB edits; intentionally one-app-per-disk, no B-tree splits/overflow handling. Musashi tracer has limited Resource/Memory Manager stubs, not full Mac OS. GPLv3-or-later declared; per-component LICENSES/NOTICE and vintage OS/ROM restrictions apply.
- https://github.com/khawkins98/classic-vibe-mac/blob/0b7a51a8b2c2fc2eda4f21a56483e210744ca7d8/README.md
- https://github.com/khawkins98/classic-vibe-mac/blob/0b7a51a8b2c2fc2eda4f21a56483e210744ca7d8/package.json

- https://github.com/khawkins98/classic-vibe-mac/blob/0b7a51a8b2c2fc2eda4f21a56483e210744ca7d8/package.json

### Identity (reviewed)

Amiga collection-management application with substantive OFS/FFS ADF generation, boot-image creation, Hunk validation and archive extraction utilities.
- https://github.com/gruetze-software/AmiDiskLab/blob/0d95ddc052533b24126e7fb8ce254392f7cc6090/README.md
- https://github.com/gruetze-software/AmiDiskLab/blob/0d95ddc052533b24126e7fb8ce254392f7cc6090/RetroDisk.Infrastructure/FileSystem/AmigaDiskImageWriter.cs

### Classification (reviewed)

Inspected 1760x512 geometry, capacity/path/symlink checks, existing startup-sequence protection and HUNK_HEADER validation. This is actual disk authoring/extraction tooling, not merely a list of third-party images. Metadata lookup uses outside services; no requests or uploads performed. MIT project license; Hst/other dependencies and original Amiga executables/assets retain separate terms.
- https://github.com/gruetze-software/AmiDiskLab/blob/0d95ddc052533b24126e7fb8ce254392f7cc6090/README.md
- https://github.com/gruetze-software/AmiDiskLab/blob/0d95ddc052533b24126e7fb8ce254392f7cc6090/RetroDisk.Infrastructure/FileSystem/AmigaDiskImageWriter.cs

### Build (reviewed)

Requires .NET10 SDK for restore/build/test; Hst.Amiga and Amiga.FileFormats.LHA dependencies pinned in csproj; packaged self-contained Windows/Linux x64 and Intel/Apple-Silicon macOS described by README. No compilation or execution performed. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.

### Identity (reviewed)

Independent semantic TypeScript/Three.js recreation based on Mark Moxon annotated Lander ARM disassembly, subsequently expanded with original Zarch-inspired gameplay.
- https://github.com/the-digital-pro/lander-game/blob/21510d152bfc869ba1560cc4fa6a63e7b0412b7a/README.md
- https://github.com/the-digital-pro/lander-game/blob/21510d152bfc869ba1560cc4fa6a63e7b0412b7a/web/package.json

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual source-referenced physics and Fourier terrain implementation, not an ARM interpreter or disassembly wrapper. Crucial qualification: physics.ts applies 30% source gravity, 60Hz integration and gentler wave-based ramp; extra pads, infection, landbots, volcanoes and turbines depart materially from original despite README fidelity wording. MIT for new implementation; source-derived meshes/constants and original Lander intellectual property have separate provenance.

### Identity (reviewed)

Substantive TypeScript browser reconstruction of Eric Graham Juggler raytracing, preserving original scene data and reconstructing 24-frame motion from historical material and later references.

Real CPU ray tracer with sphere BVH/reflection/shading/HAM encoder and reconstructed animation, not just video playback. TECHNICAL explicitly says original DAT scenes static; motion uses archival frames, Ernie Wright documentation and Meatfighter patterns. Compressed historical movie payload decoding remains future work; comparisons are evidence-guided, not pixel-perfect. No explicit license file established for new code. Preserved scene data credits Graham and AlphaPixel attribution; do not extend that grant to the entire implementation.

GPLv2-or-later per README/source; some graphics replaced specifically to retain free distribution.

Original author release of a 2018 commercial native retro game, not binary-derived reconstruction. Source contains substantive framework/gameplay and asset trees; no full dependency/asset completeness audit or retail byte match performed. PC emulator-hosted EMUBUILD variant requires external host communication; native console source itself is meaningful 68000 code, not a CPU-wrapper-only project. Repository LICENSE is GPLv3; README separately requests no distribution of unmodified game binaries and says proprietary assemblers must be obtained separately. Echo sound system has its own linked license. Report these distinctions rather than resolving legal scope.

### Identity (reviewed)

Maintained historical multi-language cross-compiler toolchain with real frontends, EM intermediate-code machinery, code generators, runtimes and cross-platform link tools.
- https://github.com/davidgiven/ack/blob/7afa32a0a0f13e865fa2e8104e442689005cd627/README
- https://github.com/davidgiven/ack/blob/7afa32a0a0f13e865fa2e8104e442689005cd627/Makefile

### Classification (reviewed)

Real maintained compiler preservation/tool development, not just historic source dump. README warns only some ancient components build and build.lua identifies current machinery; libraries roughly ANSI-C-level and ACK object format cannot be mixed with other compiler objects. Target CPU list follows supported output descriptions, not inferred from portability; no fixed host/input CPU. Root three-clause BSD-style Vrije Universiteit license; per-file/third-party components may differ.
- https://github.com/davidgiven/ack/blob/7afa32a0a0f13e865fa2e8104e442689005cd627/README
- https://github.com/davidgiven/ack/blob/7afa32a0a0f13e865fa2e8104e442689005cd627/Makefile

Documented requirements C compiler, flex/yacc, GNU Make, Lua+lua-posix, Python>=3.4 and about1GB target directory. No build/tests executed. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.

## Research event notes

Broad discovery followed platform catalogues, developer portfolios, source-preservation and tooling links, with programmatic URL screening before detailed inspection. This is a diverse bounded pass, not an exhaustive inventory of every platform. Quality and completeness caveats remain explicit; preserved source, native reconstruction, homebrew and modern tooling are distinguished. No candidate code was executed. All earlier catalogue records are preserved.
